| ID | Rule (quoted from the source; shorten only by cutting, never paraphrase) | What to grep/read for in a PHP/JS WordPress plugin or its readme | Source |

| DEV-221 | "Developers can use blueprint files in order to configure the preview environment and install dependencies (such as other plugins and themes)." | `assets/blueprints/blueprint.json` `steps` include `installPlugin` for every `Requires Plugins:` slug | Previews and Blueprints › The Plugin Preview Button |
| DEV-222 | "A valid `blueprint.json` file must be provided in a blueprints sub-directory of the plugin’s assets folder." | SVN `/assets/blueprints/blueprint.json` exists and parses as JSON | Previews and Blueprints › Enabling Plugin Previews |
| DEV-223 | "The plugin preview must be set to “public” from the plugin’s Advanced view by a committer." | Process — check: Advanced view preview toggle set to public | Previews and Blueprints › Enabling Plugin Previews |
| DEV-224 | "If a valid `blueprint.json` file is present, then the Preview button will be present for plugin committers only." | Process — check: committers test the "Test Preview" before going public | Previews and Blueprints › Enabling Plugin Previews |
| DEV-225 | "The blueprint for your plugin should be committed to the assets folder with subversion as `assets/blueprints/blueprint.json`." | Path is exactly `assets/blueprints/blueprint.json` (not in trunk or the zip) | Previews and Blueprints › Blueprints |
| DEV-226 | "Initially only the one blueprint file is supported, but we expect to allow multiple in future." | Only one `assets/blueprints/blueprint.json`; no other blueprint names | Previews and Blueprints › Blueprints |

| DEV-228 | "2. Fix any bugs in your plugin that prevent it from working in Playground." | Plugin has no hard dependency Playground lacks (e.g. MySQL-only SQL, exec/shell, outbound network at activation) | Previews and Blueprints › Using a generated Blueprint file |
| DEV-229 | "3. Download blueprint.json" | Process — check: start from the generated blueprint | Previews and Blueprints › Using a generated Blueprint file |
| DEV-230 | "4. Commit your blueprint to svn." | Process — check: `svn add assets/blueprints/blueprint.json` and commit | Previews and Blueprints › Using a generated Blueprint file |

| DEV-232 | "You must commit your blueprint.json file to your plugin's assets folder, named like this:" | File named `blueprint.json` under `assets/blueprints/` | Previews and Blueprints › Committing a Blueprint to Subversion |

| DEV-247 | "you do not need to add anyone to your plugin with more access than you're comfortable with." | Process — check: least-privilege roles | Special User Roles › Contributor |

|---|---|---|---|

| DEV-253 | "Your application password is shown only once. | DEV-267 | "Running Plugin Check  locally is a good way to catch common issues, and a prerequisite before submitting a plugin for review." | `wp plugin check <slug>` run locally before every submission | MCP Server › Review what AI produces |
