## Part B -- wordpress.org rulebook, part B: the automated checks

Compiled 2026-10-04 from first-party sources only. Plugin Check source was read from a shallow clone of `github.com/WordPress/plugin-check` trunk at commit `25dfd0f7f52f96a05d7d4f0d249d602c69cc590d` (2026-10-02); WPCS / VIPCS sniff messages from raw GitHub source; everything else by WebFetch.

## Findings that change how to audit

1. **None of the three round-2 issues is detectable by Plugin Check.** A grep of `includes/`, `phpcs-sniffs/`, `phpcs-rulesets/` and `prompts/` at trunk finds no check for `permission_callback`, `upload_mimes`, `activate_plugin(s)` / `deactivate_plugins`, `session_start` or `wp-load`. These come only from the AI pre-review. A clean Plugin Check run therefore proves nothing about them; the BOT and REST rows below have to be audited by reading the code.
2. **Plugin Check ships the naming half of the pre-review prompt.** `prompts/ai-check-prereview.md` opens "Assisting the WordPress.org Plugins Team" and asks for `possible_naming_issues`, `possible_owner_issues`, `possible_description_issues`. Its output spec says "This message will be included inside an email". That is the plugin name, owner and description part of the automated pre-review, and it is public (rows BOT-name-*).
3. **Plugin Check respects `phpcs:ignore`.** Nothing in `includes/` passes `--ignore-annotations`. The human and AI reviewers do not depend on that, as round 1 showed. Judge the plugin with annotations ignored.
4. **No experimental checks exist at trunk.** No class returns `STABILITY_EXPERIMENTAL`. `--include-experimental` exists but currently adds nothing.
5. **`Nonce_Verification_Check` exists but is not registered.** It has no slug in `Default_Check_Repository.php`, so the custom `PluginCheck.Security.VerifyNonce` sniff never runs in a normal check. `PluginCheck.Security.AIConnectorAPIKey` is in the PluginCheck standard, but no check's sniff list names it.
6. **Submission gate:** a Plugin Repo category **error** blocks submission (make/plugins 2024-10-01). `prefixing` results are always downgraded to warning severity 6. With `--ai`, only errors below severity 7 and warnings below 6 go to AI triage; anything at or above that is reported unfiltered.
7. **The release-time AI security review (since June 2026) lists its own risk patterns** (make/plugins 2026-09-09): "REST/AJAX/admin endpoints without capability checks", "Queries without `$wpdb->prepare()`", "File operations from request data", "`unserialize()` on external data", "Unprotected options/metadata modifications" (rows BOT-rel-*).

---

Source: https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/tree/trunk/includes/Checker/Checks (fetched 2026-10-04 via clone; registry in `includes/Checker/Default_Check_Repository.php`; descriptions in `docs/checks.md`). Category shown in brackets.

| ID | Rule / check (quoted or the check's own message; shorten only by cutting, never by paraphrasing) | What to grep/read for in plugin code | Source URL |

Runs sniff `WordPress.WP.I18n` with `text_domain` = plugin slug (see PCS-WordPress.WP.I18n.*). | `__(`, `_e(`, `_x(`, `_n(`, `esc_html__(`, `esc_attr__(` etc.: literal strings only, text domain literal == slug, `/* translators: */` above placeholders | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/includes/Checker/Checks/General/I18n_Usage_Check.php |
| PC-php_error_reporting | [general] "Detects runtime changes to PHP error reporting configuration or WordPress debug constants." Messages: "Detected production-time change to PHP error reporting: %s()." / "Detected production-time debug constant definition: %s." | `error_reporting(`, `ini_set( 'display_errors'`, `ini_set( 'error_reporting'`, `ini_alter(`, `define( 'WP_DEBUG'`, `WP_DEBUG_LOG`, `WP_DEBUG_DISPLAY`, `SCRIPT_DEBUG` (also `const`) | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/phpcs-sniffs/PluginCheck/Sniffs/CodeAnalysis/PHPErrorReportingSniff.php |
| PC-react_usage | [general, plugin_repo] "Detects React usage that breaks when WordPress upgrades to React 19." Codes `react_removed_api`, `inlined_react`, `inlined_react_dom`, `inlined_react_jsx_runtime`. "This file calls "ReactDOM.findDOMNode", which was removed in React 19…" | Built JS: bundled copies of react / react-dom / jsx-runtime (not externalized via `@wordpress/dependency-extraction-webpack-plugin`), `ReactDOM.render`, `findDOMNode`, `createFactory` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/includes/Checker/Checks/General/React_Usage_Check.php |

(`five_star_reviews_detected`) / "Editor blocks must define "apiVersion" 3 or higher in block.json for WordPress 7.0+ iframe editor compatibility." (`block_api_version_too_low`) | `wordpress.org/support/plugin/<slug>/reviews/?filter=5`, `#new-post` + rating; `block.json` `"apiVersion"` < 3 | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/includes/Checker/Checks/Plugin_Repo/Plugin_Content_Check.php |
| PC-wp_functions_compatibility | [plugin_repo] "Function "%1$s()" requires WordPress %2$s, but your plugin minimum supported version is WordPress %3$s." | Calls to WP functions newer than `Requires at least:` (e.g. `wp_ai_client_prompt` vs 6.x) | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/includes/Checker/Checks/Plugin_Repo/WP_Functions_Compatibility_Check.php |
| PC-nonce_verification (UNREGISTERED) | [security, plugin_repo] "Checks for proper usage of wp_verify_nonce() to prevent CSRF vulnerabilities." The class exists, but no slug is registered at trunk, so it does not run. Messages under PCS-PluginCheck.Security.VerifyNonce. | `wp_verify_nonce(` used without checking its return value, or combined with `isset() &&` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/includes/Checker/Checks/Security/Nonce_Verification_Check.php |
| PC-ai_mode | `--ai`: "Only issues with severity **below** the threshold are sent to the AI for review." Defaults: Errors 7, Warnings 6. "False positives are annotated in the results". | n/a (affects how results are triaged) | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/docs/ai-features.md |
| PC-ai-late-escaping | AI triage rule: "Escaping earlier in the code and then outputting later is not considered late escaping." "`__()`, `_e()`, `_x()` and similar i18n functions do NOT escape data." "`printf()` / `sprintf()` do NOT escape data by themselves." | `$x = esc_html( … ); … echo $x;`; `echo __(`; `printf( $fmt, $var )` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-review-late-escaping.md |
| PC-ai-nonce | AI triage rule: "AJAX handlers should use `check_ajax_referer()` or `wp_verify_nonce()`." "If the code is in a REST API callback with a proper `permission_callback`, nonce verification is not required." "Capability checks (`current_user_can()`) alone are not sufficient — nonces are still needed for form submissions." | `add_action( 'wp_ajax_` handlers: first lines `check_ajax_referer(` + `current_user_can(` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-review-nonce-verification.md |
| PC-ai-sanitization | AI triage rule: "`isset()` and `empty()` are NOT sanitization functions." "`wp_unslash()` is NOT a sanitization function by itself." "Passwords, tokens, secrets, signatures, and API keys are generally NOT eligible to be sanitized… as long as the value is only stored… or compared… and never displayed or used unsafely" | `$_POST/$_GET/$_REQUEST/$_SERVER/$_COOKIE` reads; licence-key fields | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-review-sanitization.md |
| PC-ai-direct-db | AI triage rule: "All SQL queries with variable data must use `$wpdb->prepare()`." "Column names also cannot be prepared — they should be whitelisted/validated instead." "`IN` clauses with dynamic lists need special handling with multiple placeholders." | `ORDER BY $col`, `IN ( $ids )` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-review-direct-db-queries.md |
| PC-ai-obfuscation | AI triage rule: "`eval()` usage is always flagged regardless of context." "`str_rot13()` used on executable code is obfuscation." "Base64-encoded data used for images, fonts, or non-executable content is NOT obfuscation." | `eval(`, `base64_decode(` feeding `eval`/`include`, packed JS `eval(function(p,a,c,k,e` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-review-code-obfuscation.md |
| PC-ai-updater | AI triage rule: "License key validation that gates features (not updates) is a separate concern." "Auto-update UI modifications (enabling/disabling WordPress core auto-updates) are generally acceptable." | licence-key code paths; `auto_update_plugin` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-review-plugin-updater.md |
| PC-ai-setting-sanitization | AI triage rule: "`register_setting()` should include a `sanitize_callback` argument." "Settings registered with `sanitize_option_{$option}` filter are also considered sanitized." | `register_setting(` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-review-setting-sanitization.md |
| PC-proposed-trialware (open #1313) | Not merged: "Plugins hosted on WordPress.org must be fully functional. Some plugins include functionality in the submitted codebase but restrict access to it behind license keys, trials, payment checks, quotas, 'pro' plan gates, or similar artificial limits." Codes `trialware_locked_feature_candidate` / `trialware_locked_feature_detected` | `license`, `is_pro`, `premium`, `upgrade`, `unlock`, `quota`, `trial`, credit checks gating code that ships in the zip | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/1313 |
| PC-proposed-ob_start (open #1314) | Not merged: "Using `ob_start()` without explicitly closing the buffer within the same logical flow can lead to unpredictable behaviour." | `ob_start(` without `ob_get_clean/ob_end_clean/ob_get_flush/ob_end_flush` in the same scope | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/1314 |
| PC-proposed-runtime-fatals (open #1273) | Not merged: "`require` / `require_once` on dynamic or unguarded paths"; "Calling plugin/theme integration functions without `function_exists` guard"; "Instantiating optional classes without `class_exists` guard"; "Invoking dynamic callbacks without `is_callable`" | `require_once $var`, WooCommerce/other-plugin calls without guards | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/1273 |

## Plugin Check: PHPCS sniffs
Sources: https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/phpcs-rulesets/plugin-check.ruleset.xml and the `get_args()` of each check; custom sniffs in `phpcs-sniffs/PluginCheck/Sniffs`; WPCS messages from https://github.com/WordPress/WordPress-Coding-Standards/tree/develop/WordPress/Sniffs (fetched 2026-10-04). "Run by" names the PC check.

| ID | Rule / check (quoted or the check's own message; shorten only by cutting, never by paraphrasing) | What to grep/read for in plugin code | Source URL |
|---|---|---|---|
| PCS-WordPress.DB.PreparedSQL.NotPrepared | Run by plugin_review_phpcs (error). "Use placeholders and $wpdb->prepare(); found %s" | `$wpdb->query(`, `get_results(`, `get_var(`, `get_row(`, `get_col(` whose SQL contains a variable or a function call result | https://github.com/WordPress/WordPress-Coding-Standards/blob/develop/WordPress/Sniffs/DB/PreparedSQLSniff.php |
| PCS-WordPress.DB.PreparedSQL.InterpolatedNotPrepared | plugin_review_phpcs, downgraded to warning. "Use placeholders and $wpdb->prepare(); found interpolated variable %s at %s" | `"SELECT … {$table} … $var"` passed to `$wpdb->` | same |
| PCS-WordPress.DB.PreparedSQLPlaceholders | plugin_review_phpcs. Codes: "Unsupported placeholder used in $wpdb->prepare()." / "Simple placeholders should not be quoted in the query string in $wpdb->prepare()." / "Incorrect number of replacements passed to $wpdb->prepare()." / "Replacement variables found, but no valid placeholders found in the query." / "It is not necessary to prepare a query which doesn't use variable replacement." / "SQL wildcards for a LIKE query should be passed in through a replacement parameter." / "The %i placeholder cannot be used within SQL `IN()` clauses." / "Found unescaped literal "%%" character." / "Dynamic placeholder generation should not have surrounding quotes." | `prepare(` calls: `'%s'` quoted, `%i` (needs WP 6.2), `LIKE '%…%'`, `IN (` + `implode` | https://github.com/WordPress/WordPress-Coding-Standards/blob/develop/WordPress/Sniffs/DB/PreparedSQLPlaceholdersSniff.php |
| PCS-WordPress.Security.NonceVerification | plugin_review_phpcs, as warning. "Processing form data without nonce verification." `Missing` for `$_POST`/`$_FILES`, `Recommended` for `$_GET`/`$_REQUEST` | Every `$_POST`, `$_FILES`, `$_GET`, `$_REQUEST` read; is `wp_verify_nonce`/`check_admin_referer`/`check_ajax_referer` earlier in the same function? | https://github.com/WordPress/WordPress-Coding-Standards/blob/develop/WordPress/Sniffs/Security/NonceVerificationSniff.php |
| PCS-WordPress.Security.ValidatedSanitizedInput.InputNotSanitized | plugin_review_phpcs, warning. "Detected usage of a non-sanitized input variable: %s" | `$_POST[...]` etc. not wrapped in `sanitize_*`/`absint`/cast | https://github.com/WordPress/WordPress-Coding-Standards/blob/develop/WordPress/Sniffs/Security/ValidatedSanitizedInputSniff.php |
| PCS-WordPress.Security.ValidatedSanitizedInput.InputNotValidated | "Detected usage of a possibly undefined superglobal array index: %s. Check that the array index exists before using it." | `$_POST['x']` without `isset(` / `! empty(` | same |
| PCS-WordPress.Security.ValidatedSanitizedInput.MissingUnslash | "%s not unslashed before sanitization. Use wp_unslash() or similar" | `sanitize_text_field( $_POST['x'] )` without `wp_unslash(` | same |
| PCS-WordPress.Security.ValidatedSanitizedInput.InputNotValidatedNotSanitized | "Detected usage of a non-sanitized, non-validated input variable %s: %s" | Superglobals interpolated into strings: `"…{$_GET['x']}…"` | same |
| PCS-Generic.PHP.BacktickOperator | plugin_review_phpcs, severity 7. Prohibits the backtick operator. | `` `shell cmd` `` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/phpcs-rulesets/plugin-check.ruleset.xml |
| PCS-PluginCheck.CodeAnalysis.Heredoc | plugin_review_phpcs, sev 7. "Use of heredoc syntax (<<<) is not allowed; use standard strings or inline HTML instead" (nowdoc stays allowed per #1286) | `<<<` not followed by `'` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/phpcs-sniffs/PluginCheck/Sniffs/CodeAnalysis/HeredocSniff.php |
| PCS-PluginCheck.CodeAnalysis.ShortURL | plugin_review_phpcs, sev 6, warning. "Short URL detected (%s). Use full URLs instead of URL shorteners." | `bit.ly`, `goo.gl`, `tinyurl.com`, `ow.ly`, `is.gd`, `cutt.ly`, `rb.gy`, `shorturl.at`, `tiny.cc`, `v.gd`, `adf.ly`, `bit.do`, `clck.ru`, `lc.chat`, `polr.me`, `s2r.co`, `short.link`, `soo.gd`, `df.ly` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/phpcs-sniffs/PluginCheck/Sniffs/CodeAnalysis/ShortURLSniff.php |
| PCS-Generic.PHP.DiscourageGoto.Found | error sev 7: "The "goto" language construct should not be used." | `goto ` | plugin-check.ruleset.xml |
| PCS-WordPress.PHP.DevelopmentFunctions | warning. "%s() found. Debug code should not normally be used in production." / "%s() can lead to full path disclosure." | `error_log(`, `var_dump(`, `var_export(`, `print_r(`, `trigger_error(`, `set_error_handler(`, `debug_backtrace(`, `debug_print_backtrace(`, `wp_debug_backtrace_summary(`, `error_reporting(`, `phpinfo(` | https://github.com/WordPress/WordPress-Coding-Standards/blob/develop/WordPress/Sniffs/PHP/DevelopmentFunctionsSniff.php |

| PCS-WordPress.WP.AlternativeFunctions.curl | type error. "Using cURL functions is highly discouraged. | PCS-WordPress.WP.AlternativeFunctions (excluded) | Excluded in the PC ruleset: `json_encode_json_encode`, `file_get_contents_file_get_contents`, `file_system_operations_file_put_contents`. Consider using check_admin_referer() instead, which exits on failure." / "Unsafe use of wp_verify_nonce() with isset() and AND operator. If isset() is false, the nonce is never checked. Use OR operator instead: if ( ! isset(...) \|\| ! wp_verify_nonce(...) )" / "Unsafe use of wp_verify_nonce() with !isset() and AND operator." / "Possibly unsafe use of wp_verify_nonce() with OR operator. If the condition before \|\| is true, the nonce is never checked." | `wp_verify_nonce(` as a bare statement; `isset( $_POST['n'] ) && wp_verify_nonce(`; `$a \|\| wp_verify_nonce(` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/phpcs-sniffs/PluginCheck/Sniffs/Security/VerifyNonceSniff.php |
| PCS-PluginCheck.Security.AIConnectorAPIKey | In the PluginCheck standard; no check's sniff list names it at trunk. "Your plugin reads WordPress AI Connector API keys directly from the options table. Plugins should not access connector credentials directly. Use the WordPress AI Client instead." | `get_option( 'connectors_ai_*_api_key' )`, `get_options( [...] )` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/phpcs-sniffs/PluginCheck/Sniffs/Security/AIConnectorAPIKeySniff.php |

## wordpress.org internal code-analysis ruleset (the "WPORG Code Analysis" scanner)
Source: https://github.com/WordPress/wporg-code-analysis/blob/trunk/MinimalPluginStandard/ruleset.xml (fetched 2026-10-04). Plugin Check issue #968 says this repo is "currently scanning plugins". Repo sniffs: `MinimalPluginStandard/Sniffs/DirectDBSniff.php`, `OutputEscapingSniff.php`, `VerifyNonceSniff.php`.

| ID | Rule / check (quoted or the check's own message; shorten only by cutting, never by paraphrasing) | What to grep/read for in plugin code | Source URL |
|---|---|---|---|
| PCS-WCA-Generic.PHP.DiscourageGoto | error: "The "goto" language construct should not be used." | `goto` | https://github.com/WordPress/wporg-code-analysis/blob/trunk/MinimalPluginStandard/ruleset.xml |
| PCS-WCA-Squiz.PHP.Eval.Discouraged | error: "eval() is a security risk so not allowed." | `eval(` | same |
| PCS-WCA-WordPress.PHP.RestrictedPHPFunctions | `create_function()`: "internally performs an eval()… these must not be used." | `create_function(` | same |
| PCS-WCA-WordPress.Security.SafeRedirect | warning: "wp_redirect() found." | `wp_redirect(` | same |
| PCS-WCA-WordPress.DB.PreparedSQLPlaceholders | warning (LikeWithoutWildcards, UnnecessaryPrepare, UnfinishedPrepare, MissingReplacements excluded) | `$wpdb->prepare(` | same |
| PCS-WCA-Generic.PHP.DisallowShortOpenTag | warning | `<?` | same |
| PCS-WCA-Generic.PHP.DisallowAlternativePHPTags | Alternative PHP open tags not allowed. | `<%` | same |
| PCS-WCA-Internal.Tokenizer.Exception | "File appears to be minified and cannot be processed. The non-minified file must be included too." | minified PHP without source | same |
| PCS-WCA-WordPress.Security.EscapeOutput | warning. Treated as auto-escaped (no flag): `__`, `_e`, `_x`, `wp_create_nonce`, `plugin_dir_url`, `plugins_url`, `admin_url`, `sanitize_text_field`, `sanitize_textarea_field`, `get_locale`, `get_bloginfo` | `echo` of anything else | same |
| PCS-WCA-Generic.PHP.BacktickOperator | Prohibits backticks. | `` ` `` | same |
| PCS-WCA-Generic.PHP.ForbiddenFunctions | Private functions: `_cleanup_header_comment`, `_get_plugin_data_markup_translate`, `_transition_post_status`, `_wp_post_revision_fields`, `do_shortcode_tag`, `get_post_type_labels`, `wp_get_sidebars_widgets`, `wp_get_widget_defaults`, `wp_unregister_GLOBALS` | those calls | same |
| PCS-WCA-WordPress.NamingConventions.PrefixAllGlobals | "Verify that everything in the global namespace is prefixed." | global symbols | same |
| PCS-WCA-WordPress.WP.Deprecated* / DiscouragedConstants / DeprecatedParameterValues | DeprecatedClasses, DeprecatedFunctions (warning), DeprecatedParameters (warning), DiscouragedConstants (warning), DeprecatedParameterValues | deprecated API | same |
| PCS-WCA-DirectDB / OutputEscaping / VerifyNonce | Custom scanner sniffs. PC's `DirectDBSniff` and `VerifyNonceSniff` were ported from these (PC #1075, #1072). | `$wpdb->` escaping; echo; `wp_verify_nonce` logic | https://github.com/WordPress/wporg-code-analysis/tree/trunk/MinimalPluginStandard/Sniffs |

## Automated pre-review (AUTOPREREVIEW) and review-email issue titles
Sources: https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ (the page whose headings the review emails use; "Changing Active Plugins" in the round-2 email is a heading there word for word), https://make.wordpress.org/plugins/handbook/performing-reviews/review-checklist/, https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/detailed-plugin-guidelines/, Plugin Check prompts and issues, make/plugins posts, all fetched 2026-10-04. Status: **VERIFIED** = the title or its text is quoted at the URL; **VERIFIED (email)** = known only from the email the caller received; **UNVERIFIED** = no public quote found.

| ID | Rule / check (quoted or the check's own message; shorten only by cutting, never by paraphrasing) | What to grep/read for in plugin code | Source URL |
|---|---|---|---|
| BOT-changing-active-plugins | VERIFIED. "Changing Active Plugins": "It is not allowed for plugins to change the activation status of other plugins, this is an action that must be performed by the user." (Points to WP 6.5 Plugin Dependencies.) The round-2 email applied it to **self**-deactivation. | `deactivate_plugins(`, `activate_plugin(`, `activate_plugins(`, `update_option( 'active_plugins'`, `active_sitewide_plugins`, `plugin_basename( __FILE__ )` passed to deactivate, a watchdog/crash handler that turns the plugin off, `register_activation_hook` callbacks that deactivate on a failed requirement check | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ |
| BOT-permission-callback | VERIFIED (email) as "Check permission_callback". No public quote of the email text found. Basis: REST-1…REST-9 and BOT-rel-endpoints. Round 2: `manage_categories` was too weak for what the route writes. | Every `register_rest_route(`: does `permission_callback` require the capability that matches the **write** (`upload_files`/`edit_post( $id )`/`delete_post`/`manage_options`), not a neighbouring one? No `__return_true` on writes. Ownership checks per object id. | https://developer.wordpress.org/rest-api/extending-the-rest-api/adding-custom-endpoints/ |
| BOT-insecure-mime | VERIFIED (email) as "Adding insecure MIME types". No public email text found. Nearest official text: "ALLOW_UNFILTERED_UPLOADS is not allowed." "If you need to add a specific file that is not in the list and that won't represent a security risk, you can do so using the upload_mimes filter." | `add_filter( 'upload_mimes'`, `mime_types`, `wp_check_filetype_and_ext` filters: the added list must be fixed and literal, with no user-supplied or option-driven extensions, nothing executable (`php`, `phtml`, `phar`, `js`, `html`, `svg` without sanitising), and no `'*'`/wildcard mapping | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ |
| BOT-undocumented-3rd-party | VERIFIED. "Undocumented use of a 3rd Party or external service" … "Remember, this is for your own legal protection. Use of services must be upfront and well documented." Readme must explain the service, link it, and include "links to the services' terms of use and/or privacy policies." | `wp_remote_get(`, `wp_remote_post(`, `wp_remote_request(`, `wp_safe_remote_*`, `fetch(`, `XMLHttpRequest`, `navigator.sendBeacon`, hard-coded hosts; readme `== External services ==` naming each host, what is sent, when, ToS + privacy links | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/475 ; https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ |
| BOT-calling-files-remotely | VERIFIED. "Calling files remotely": "Offloading images, js, css, and other scripts to your servers or any remote service (like Google, MaxCDN, jQuery.com etc) is disallowed. When you call remote data you introduce an unnecessary dependency on another site." | see PC-offloading_files; also `<img src="https://`, remote fonts other than Google Fonts | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/486 |
| BOT-libraries-in-core | VERIFIED. "Including Libraries Already In Core": "plugins may not include those libraries in their own code, but instead must use the versions of those libraries packaged with WordPress." | see PC-file_type/library_core_files; `wp_enqueue_script( 'jquery'` dependency instead of a bundled copy | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ |
| BOT-direct-file-access | VERIFIED. "Allowing Direct File Access to plugin files" … "Add `if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) exit;` at top of PHP files." | see PC-direct_file_access | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ |
| BOT-generic-names | VERIFIED. "Generic function/class/define/namespace/option names"; "All plugins must have unique function names, namespaces, defines, class and option names." "Don't try to use two (2) or three (3) letter prefixes anymore." Reserved: `__`, `wp_`, `_`. Wrapping in `if (!function_exists('NAME'))` does not count as protection. | Option names in `get_option`/`update_option`/`add_option`/`register_setting`, transient names, `define(`, global functions, classes, namespace, AJAX action names `wp_ajax_*`, REST namespace, CPT/taxonomy keys, user/post meta keys, cron hooks, script handles | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ ; https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/523 |
| BOT-sanitize | VERIFIED. "Input data must be Sanitized, Validated, and Escaped on output." Check #582: "when the plugin includes POST/GET/REQUEST/FILE calls, must be sanitized, validated and escaped." | all superglobals, `file_get_contents( 'php://input' )`, `$request->get_param(`, `$request->get_json_params()` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/582 |
| BOT-sanitize-escape-confusion | VERIFIED. "Sanitize: Confusion about escape and sanitize functions": escape functions cannot sanitize. | `esc_html( $_POST[...] )` used as input sanitizing; `esc_url_raw` on output | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ |
| BOT-sanitize-filter-functions | VERIFIED. "Sanitize: Using filter functions to sanitize": leaving the filter empty "applies "FILTER_DEFAULT" which doesn't sanitize." | `filter_input(`, `filter_var(` without a sanitising filter | same |
| BOT-sanitize-nonces | VERIFIED. "Sanitize: Nonces": "When checking a nonce using wp_verify_nonce you will need to sanitize the input using wp_unslash AND sanitize_text_field." Pattern: `if ( ! isset( $_POST['prefix_nonce'] ) \|\| ! wp_verify_nonce( sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash ( $_POST['prefix_nonce'] ) ) , 'prefix_nonce' )` | `wp_verify_nonce( $_POST[` / `$_REQUEST[` raw | same |
| BOT-nonces-user-permissions | UNVERIFIED as a title ("Nonces and user permissions"). The underlying rules are VERIFIED at SEC-9…SEC-13 and PC-ai-nonce. | `wp_ajax_*` handlers, `admin_post_*`, `admin_init` form handlers: nonce + `current_user_can` | https://developer.wordpress.org/apis/security/nonces/ |
| BOT-processing-whole-input | VERIFIED. "Processing the whole input": "We strongly recommend you never attempt to process the whole $_POST/$_REQUEST/$_GET stack." | `foreach ( $_POST`, `array_map( 'sanitize_text_field', $_POST )`, `wp_unslash( $_POST )`, `$request->get_params()` saved whole, `update_option( 'x', $_POST )` | same |
| BOT-escape | VERIFIED. "Variables and options must be escaped when echo'd". Escape late, when outputting. | see PC-late_escaping | same |
| BOT-escape-esc_url_raw | VERIFIED. "Escape: Use of esc_url_raw": sanitises URLs for the database or redirects; use `esc_url` for output. | `echo esc_url_raw(` | same |
| BOT-escape-__ | VERIFIED. "Escape: Use of __": `__` "retrieves translations without escaping." | `echo __(`, `'…' . __( … ) . '…'` echoed | same |
| BOT-escape-_e | VERIFIED. "Escape: Use of _e and _ex": use `esc_html_e`, `esc_attr_e`. | `_e(`, `_ex(` | same |
| BOT-escape-json | VERIFIED. "Escape: Use json_encode": use `wp_json_encode`, and don't pass options in the second parameter that bypass escaping. | `echo json_encode(`, `wp_json_encode( $x, JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES` in `<script>` | same |
| BOT-escape-html | VERIFIED. "Escape: HTML": use `wp_kses_post` or `wp_kses` for HTML. | HTML strings echoed through `esc_html` or not at all | same |
| BOT-escape-sanitizing-functions | VERIFIED. "Escape: Using sanitizing functions": sanitizing functions cannot escape. | `echo sanitize_text_field(` | same |
| BOT-file-uploader | VERIFIED. "When plugins use `move_uploaded_file()`, they exclude their uploads from the built-in checks and balances with WordPress's functions." Use `wp_handle_upload`. | `move_uploaded_file(`, `$_FILES` written by hand, `copy( $_FILES` | same |
| BOT-unfiltered-uploads | VERIFIED. "ALLOW_UNFILTERED_UPLOADS is not allowed. Setting this constant to true will allow the user to upload any type of file (including PHP and other executables), creating serious potential security risks." | `ALLOW_UNFILTERED_UPLOADS`, `unfiltered_upload` cap granted via `map_meta_cap`/`user_has_cap` | same |
| BOT-libraries-dev-versions | VERIFIED. "Libraries: Using development versions": "We do not recommend you use the beta version of a library unless it has features required by your plugin." | `composer.lock` / bundled JS versions with `-beta`, `-rc`, `dev-` | same |
| BOT-libraries-not-maintained | VERIFIED. "Libraries that are no longer maintained are not permitted." | Abandoned packages in `composer.json`/`package.json` | same |
| BOT-libraries-out-of-date | VERIFIED. "Libraries: Out of Date": "Please upgrade to the latest stable version for better support and security." | Versions of every bundled library vs latest stable | same |
| BOT-unsafe-sql | VERIFIED. "WPDB: Unsafe SQL calls": "When making database calls, it's highly important to protect your code from SQL injection vulnerabilities." Use `$wpdb` and `prepare()`. | see PCS-WordPress.DB.PreparedSQL.* | same |
| BOT-sql-array-placeholders | VERIFIED. "WPDB: Arrays of placeholders": one placeholder per array item using `array_fill` and `implode`. | `IN (` built with `implode( ',', $ids )` | same |
| BOT-heredoc | VERIFIED. "Not use HEREDOC-NOWDOC": "Do not use HEREDOC or NOWDOC syntax in your plugins." (PC #1286 keeps nowdoc allowed in Plugin Check.) | `<<<` | same |
| BOT-php-short-tags | VERIFIED. "PHP: Do not use Short Tags" … "The risk here is way higher than the benefits, which is why we don't permit their use." | `<?` / `<?=` | same |
| BOT-php-settings-globally | VERIFIED. "Don't Force Set PHP Settings Globally." #587: "Having defines like ini_set('memory_limit', '-1'); run globally (like on init or in the __construct() part of the code) means developers will be running that for everything on the site… Functions not allowed run in global: `set_time_limit, ini_set, ini_alter, dl`" | `set_time_limit(`, `ini_set(`, `ini_alter(`, `dl(`, `wp_raise_memory_limit(` outside the one function that needs it; in a crash watchdog check `register_shutdown_function`/`set_error_handler`/`ini_set` on load | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/587 |
| BOT-php-timezone | VERIFIED. "PHP: Setting a default timezone": "This is rarely a good idea." | `date_default_timezone_set(` | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ |
| BOT-php-error-reporting | VERIFIED. "Don't Use Error Reporting in Production Code." "Error_reporting() should not be set permanently in plugins, as it interferes with site debugging efforts." | `error_reporting(`, `ini_set( 'display_errors'`, `@ini_set`, `set_error_handler(` global | same |
| BOT-debug-code | VERIFIED. #657: "During development it is common to use functions such as error_log or var_dump to check the value or state of variables. Sometimes we leave this code by mistake in our final version, please take a look at it." | `error_log(`, `var_dump(`, `print_r(`, `console.log(` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/657 |
| BOT-main-file-convention | VERIFIED. "The main file of the plugin has a name that does not follow the convention." | main file name == folder slug (`slug/slug.php`) | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ |
| BOT-incomplete-headers | VERIFIED. "Your headers are either missing or incomplete." | see PC-plugin_header_fields | same |
| BOT-incomplete-readme | VERIFIED. "Your readme is either missing or incomplete." | see PC-plugin_readme | same |
| BOT-no-gpl-license | VERIFIED. "No GPL-compatible license declared": "It is necessary to declare the license of this plugin." | `License:` in header + readme; bundled assets' licences | same |
| BOT-stable-tag | VERIFIED. "Incorrect Stable Tag": "Your Stable Tag does not match the Plugin Version." | `Stable tag:` == `Version:` | same |
| BOT-license-mismatch | VERIFIED. "Declared license mismatched": "When declaring the license for this plugin, it has to be the same." | readme vs header `License:` | same |
| BOT-curl | VERIFIED. "Using CURL Instead of HTTP API" … "WordPress comes with an extensive HTTP API that should be used instead." CURL inside third-party vendor libraries stays permitted. | `curl_` | same |
| BOT-wp-enqueue | VERIFIED. "Use wp_enqueue commands": "Your plugin is not correctly including JS and/or CSS." | `<script`, `<style`, `<link rel="stylesheet"` echoed; inline JS should use `wp_add_inline_script`/`wp_localize_script` | same |
| BOT-i18n-variables | VERIFIED. "Internationalization: Using variables": "Don't use variables or defines as text, context or text domain parameters." | see PCS-WordPress.WP.I18n.NonSingularStringLiteral* | same |
| BOT-text-domain | VERIFIED (Plugin Check message). "Mismatched text domain. Expected '%s' but got %s." / header "…does not match the slug." | `Text Domain:` and every gettext domain == slug | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/includes/Checker/Checks/Plugin_Repo/Plugin_Header_Fields_Check.php |
| BOT-load-plugin-textdomain | VERIFIED (Plugin Check message, #909 title "Using load_plugin_textdomain() for loading the plugin translations is not needed for WordPress.org directory since WordPress 4.6") | `load_plugin_textdomain(` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/includes/Checker/Checks/Plugin_Repo/Plugin_Review_PHPCS_Check.php |
| BOT-contributors | VERIFIED (Plugin Check message). "The "%s" field should only contain WordPress.org usernames. Remember that usernames are case-sensitive." | readme `Contributors:` | Plugin_Readme_Check.php |
| BOT-tested-up-to | VERIFIED (Plugin Check message; #481 "Check: Tested up to Latest version of WordPress"). | readme `Tested up to:` | Plugin_Readme_Check.php |
| BOT-update-checker | VERIFIED. "Including An Update Checker / Changing Updates functionality": "We do not permit plugins to phone home to other servers for updates, as we are providing that service for you with WordPress.org hosting." | see PC-plugin_updater; Pro add-on update code must not ship in the free zip | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/common-issues/ |
| BOT-unneeded-folders | VERIFIED. "Included Unneeded Folders": remove development tools, vendor folders, demos, and unit tests; "Keep configuration files" like `composer.json` for review. | `tests/`, `node_modules/`, `.github/`, `docs/`, `bin/`, `phpunit.xml`, `.claude/` in the zip | same |
| BOT-not-permitted-files | VERIFIED. "Not permitted files": plugins typically contain PHP, JS, CSS, multimedia, data files. | anything else in the zip (`.sh`, `.exe`, `.log`, `.sql`, `.env`) | same |
| BOT-premium-source | VERIFIED. "Including code from a "premium" source": "Some premium libraries are specifically not permitted to be included in free (WordPress.org hosted) plugins." | bundled premium libraries/add-ons | same |
| BOT-gpl-non-compatible | VERIFIED. "GPL: Non-GPL compatible Code Included": "It's required that all code be compatible with the GPLv2 (or later) license." | licence headers of bundled code/fonts/images | same |
| BOT-gpl-no-public-source | VERIFIED. "GPL: No publicly documented resource for your compressed content". | minified/compiled JS/CSS without source in zip or a readme link to the source repo | same |

| BOT-saving-in-plugin-folder | VERIFIED. #665: "Saving data in the plugin folder and/or asking users to edit/write to plugin." Checklist: "No saving content locally to the plugin folder, as it is deleted on upgrades." | see PC-write_file; logs/caches/crash markers written under `plugin_dir_path( __FILE__ )` | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/665 |
| BOT-determine-locations | VERIFIED as a checklist item: "Using WordPress content directory functions to determine locations of folders and files." 'wp-content'`, `dirname( __FILE__ ) . '/../../'`; use `plugin_dir_path`, `plugins_url`, `wp_upload_dir`, `content_url` | https://make.wordpress.org/plugins/handbook/performing-reviews/review-checklist/ ; https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/plugin-basics/determining-plugin-and-content-directories/ |
| BOT-uninstall | Plugin Check: "Check for %s constant missing." (WP_UNINSTALL_PLUGIN). Title UNVERIFIED. | `uninstall.php` guard; `register_uninstall_hook` | Plugin_Uninstall_Check.php |
| BOT-trialware | VERIFIED. Guideline 5 "Trialware is not permitted.": "Plugins may not contain functionality that is restricted or locked, only to be made available by payment or upgrade." "Functionality may not be disabled after a trial period or quota is met." | licence-key checks around code that ships in the free zip, credit/quota gates that disable bundled features, `is_pro()`/`has_licence()` branches | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/detailed-plugin-guidelines/ ; https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/issues/1313 |
| BOT-serviceware | VERIFIED. Guideline 6: "The service itself must provide functionality of substance and be clearly documented in the readme file." | BOT-tracking-consent | VERIFIED. Guideline 7: "plugins may not contact external servers without explicit and authorized consent." Checklist: "Don't phone home without informed user consent." "Collection of user data must be 'opt-in' only and have the relevant option set to disabled by default." | remote calls on activation/`admin_init`/cron before an opt-in; telemetry; options defaulting to on | same; https://make.wordpress.org/plugins/handbook/performing-reviews/review-checklist/ |
| BOT-executable-code | VERIFIED. Guideline 8: "Executing outside code within a plugin when not acting as a service is not allowed." No installing plugins/themes from non-wordpress.org servers; no premium installs; admin iframes disallowed. Remote code / eval / base64 as a title is UNVERIFIED. | `eval(`, `include` of a downloaded file, `base64_decode(` → execution, `Plugin_Upgrader` with an external URL, `<iframe` in admin pages | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/detailed-plugin-guidelines/ |
| BOT-hijack-admin | VERIFIED. Guideline 11 "Plugins should not hijack the admin dashboard." Site-wide notices must be dismissible; "Error messages must include resolution info"; upgrade prompts used sparingly. Hiding other admin notices as a title is UNVERIFIED. | `admin_notices` callbacks without `is-dismissible`/conditions; `remove_all_actions( 'admin_notices' )`; CSS hiding `.notice`; external menu links (PC-external_admin_menu_links) | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/wordpress-org/detailed-plugin-guidelines/ |
| BOT-wp-load | VERIFIED (checklist, Not Permitted): "Calling `wp-load` directly to gain access to core functions." | `require.*wp-load.php`, `wp-config.php`, `wp-blog-header.php`, standalone PHP endpoints in the plugin dir | https://make.wordpress.org/plugins/handbook/performing-reviews/review-checklist/ |
| BOT-wp-debug-clean | VERIFIED (checklist): "No errors when run with `WP_DEBUG` set to true." | run with `WP_DEBUG`, `WP_DEBUG_LOG`; notices, deprecations, `_doing_it_wrong` (e.g. missing `permission_callback`) | same |
| BOT-url-shorteners | VERIFIED (checklist): "Do not use URL shorteners." | see PCS-PluginCheck.CodeAnalysis.ShortURL | same |
| BOT-third-party-ads | VERIFIED (checklist): "Third Party Ads are not permitted due to tracking." "No analytics or tracking by third parties." | GA/GTM/pixel snippets in admin | same |
| BOT-credit-links | VERIFIED (checklist): "Forward facing links (including credit links, powered by, and ads) must be optional and not active by default." | front-end "powered by" output | same |
| BOT-minification | VERIFIED (checklist): "No minification of scripts or files unless the original files are also provided." / "…(for example, do not use `p,a,c,k,e,r`)." | `*.min.js` without source; `eval(function(p,a,c,k,e` | same |

| BOT-service-iframe | VERIFIED (checklist, Serviceware): "Connectivity to the service is **not** performed via an iframe in the dashboard (APIs are recommended)." "Data transmission is secure and sanitized." | `<iframe` in admin; `http://` endpoints; `sslverify => false` | same |
| BOT-raw-code-input | VERIFIED (checklist, Subject Matter): "Plugins that allow users to paste in raw JS/CSS/HTML/PHP without sanitization or security." | settings that store/echo raw HTML/JS without `unfiltered_html` cap check | same |
| BOT-include-plugins-themes | VERIFIED (checklist): "Don't include other plugins wholesale. A plugin can be required but not included or auto-installed." | bundled plugin zips, TGMPA auto-install | same |
| BOT-custom-post-types-prefix | Partly VERIFIED. Checklist: "Use of a unique prefix for everything the plugin defines in the public namespace (options, functions, global variables, constants, post meta, etc.)." No public text names post types/taxonomies, so the CPT-specific title is UNVERIFIED. | `register_post_type( '`, `register_taxonomy( '` keys, meta keys, shortcode tags, block names | same |
| BOT-sessions | UNVERIFIED. Nothing about `session_start` found in any fetched source. | `session_start(`, `$_SESSION` | n/a |
| BOT-five-star | VERIFIED (Plugin Check): "Linking directly to 5 stars reviews is not allowed." | review-request notices | Plugin_Content_Check.php |
| BOT-name-generic | VERIFIED (pre-review naming prompt). "Names that are one or few words that do not describe sufficiently what the plugin does." "…an original name… should be placed at the beginning of the name, not at the end." | `Plugin Name:`, readme name | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-check-prereview.md |
| BOT-name-unrelated | VERIFIED (prompt). "The name is not related with what the plugin does" | name vs description | same |
| BOT-name-keyword-stuffing | VERIFIED (prompt). "Keyword stuffing is not allowed." | name, short description, tags | same |
| BOT-name-too-similar | VERIFIED (prompt). "Use of a name too similar to another published plugin." | compare with existing directory names (EML, FileBird, Media Library Folders…) | same; https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-check-similar-name.md |
| BOT-name-trademark | VERIFIED (prompt). "Use of trademarks or other project names in a way that can be confused (when they are not the owners of the trademark)"; allowed after "for" / "with". Banned anywhere: Facebook, WhatsApp, Instagram, Threads, Oculus, WordPress, "WP" standalone, Trustpilot, Binance Pay. Discouraged: "plugin" (redundant), best/#1/First/Perfect/The most, "free", Gutenberg. Portmanteau "-Press" is treated as the WordPress mark. | name, slug, banners | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-check-prereview.md |
| BOT-name-owner | VERIFIED (prompt output field `possible_owner_issues` / `owner_explanation`: "Explain why the owner is ok or has issues.") | author / w.org account vs brand in name | https://github.com/WordPress/plugin-check/blob/trunk/prompts/ai-check-prereview-output.md |
| BOT-description-superlatives | VERIFIED (prompt). "Describing the plugin as better than other plugins, the best overall solution or the unique solution is not allowed. This includes expressions such as 'Best than...', 'Best plugin for...', '#1 plugin for...', 'The most ... plugin for ...', 'First option for users', 'The only plugin for ...'" | readme description, short description, FAQ, plugin header Description | same |
| BOT-rel-endpoints | VERIFIED (release-time AI review, 2026-09-09, "Common Risk Patterns"): "REST/AJAX/admin endpoints without capability checks" | `register_rest_route`, `wp_ajax_`, `wp_ajax_nopriv_`, `admin_post_`, `admin_init` handlers: `current_user_can` present and matching the action | https://make.wordpress.org/plugins/2026/09/09/automated-security-review-for-plugin-releases/ |
| BOT-rel-prepare | VERIFIED (same post): "Queries without `$wpdb->prepare()`" | `$wpdb->` | same |
| BOT-rel-file-ops | VERIFIED (same post): "File operations from request data" | `unlink`, `wp_delete_file`, `rename`, `copy`, `file_put_contents`, `fopen`, `wp_handle_sideload`, `download_url` with paths/URLs from `$_REQUEST`/`$request` (path traversal, SSRF) | same |
| BOT-rel-unserialize | VERIFIED (same post): "`unserialize()` on external data" | `unserialize(`, `maybe_unserialize(` on remote responses, cookies, request data | same |
| BOT-rel-options-meta | VERIFIED (same post): "Unprotected options/metadata modifications" | `update_option(`, `delete_option(`, `update_post_meta(`, `update_user_meta(`, `wp_update_attachment_metadata(` reachable from a request without capability + nonce | same |

## Security handbook (Common APIs) and plugin security pages
Sources: https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/security/ (stub: "This content has been moved to the Security page in the Common APIs Handbook."), sub-pages `checking-user-capabilities/` (content), `data-validation/`, `securing-input/`, `securing-output/`, `nonces/` (all stubs pointing to apis/security). Content fetched from https://developer.wordpress.org/apis/security/ and sub-pages, 2026-10-04.

| ID | Rule / check (quoted or the check's own message; shorten only by cutting, never by paraphrasing) | What to grep/read for in plugin code | Source URL |
|---|---|---|---|
| SEC-1 | "Don't trust user input, third-party APIs, or data in your database without verification." | remote API responses (the AI service's alt text, folder names) echoed or stored without sanitising | https://developer.wordpress.org/apis/security/ |
| SEC-2 | "Always make sure to _validate_ and _sanitize_ user input before using it, and to _escape_ on output." | all inputs/outputs | same |
| SEC-3 | "As you build a plugin, make sure to run your code only when the current user has the necessary capabilities." | every state-changing handler: `current_user_can(` | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/security/checking-user-capabilities/ |
| SEC-4 | "If your plugin allows users to submit data—be it on the Admin or the Public side—it should check for User Capabilities." | `wp_ajax_nopriv_*`, front-end forms, REST routes | https://developer.wordpress.org/apis/security/user-roles-and-capabilities/ |
| SEC-5 | Example "Restricted to a Specific Capability": `current_user_can( 'edit_others_posts' )` checked before registering the hooks that trash posts. Use the capability that matches the action. | capability string vs the action it guards (`delete_post`, `upload_files`, `manage_options`, `edit_post`) | https://developer.wordpress.org/plugins/security/checking-user-capabilities/ |
| SEC-6 | "Data validation should be performed as early as possible." | top of handlers | https://developer.wordpress.org/apis/security/data-validation/ |
| SEC-7 | Safelist: "Accept data only from a finite list of known and trusted values." Blocklist: "Reject data from finite list of known untrusted values. This is very rarely a good idea." Plus "Format Detection" and "Format Correction". | `in_array( $x, $allowed, true )`; enum params (orderby, mode, mime type) | same |
| SEC-8 | Validation functions: `balanceTags()`/`force_balance_tags()`, `count()`, `in_array()`, `is_email()`, `is_array()`, `mb_strlen()`/`strlen()`, `preg_match()`/`strpos()`, `sanitize_html_class()`, `tag_escape()`, `term_exists()`, `username_exists()`, `validate_file()` | `validate_file(` on any path from input | same |
| SEC-9 | "Untrusted data comes from many sources (users, third party sites, even your own database!) and all of it needs to be checked before it's used." | DB values echoed | https://developer.wordpress.org/apis/security/sanitizing/ |
| SEC-10 | "Validation is preferred over sanitization because validation is more specific." | | same |
| SEC-11 | Sanitization functions: `sanitize_email()`, `sanitize_file_name()`, `sanitize_hex_color()`, `sanitize_hex_color_no_hash()`, `sanitize_html_class()`, `sanitize_key()`, `sanitize_meta()`, `sanitize_mime_type()`, `sanitize_option()`, `sanitize_sql_orderby()`, `sanitize_term()`, `sanitize_term_field()`, `sanitize_text_field()`, `sanitize_textarea_field()`, `sanitize_title()`, `sanitize_title_for_query()`, `sanitize_title_with_dashes()`, `sanitize_user()`, `sanitize_url()`, `wp_kses()`, `wp_kses_post()` | each input uses the type-specific one (`sanitize_file_name` for uploads, `sanitize_mime_type` for MIME values) | same |
| SEC-12 | "It is best to do the output escaping as late as possible, ideally as data is being outputted." Exception: escape while building, and suffix variables `_escaped`, `_safe` or `_clean`. | `echo $html` built earlier | https://developer.wordpress.org/apis/security/escaping/ |
| SEC-13 | Escaping functions: `esc_html()` "Use anytime an HTML element encloses a section of data being displayed."; `esc_js()` "Use for inline Javascript."; `esc_url()` "Use on all URLs, including those in the src and href attributes"; `esc_url_raw()` "Use when storing a URL in the database or in other cases where non-encoded URLs are needed."; `esc_xml()`; `esc_attr()` "Use on everything else that's printed into an HTML element's attribute."; `esc_textarea()`; `wp_kses()`; `wp_kses_post()`; `wp_kses_data()` | right function per context (`src`/`href` → `esc_url`, attributes → `esc_attr`) | same |
| SEC-14 | Escaping with localization: `esc_html__()`, `esc_html_e()`, `esc_html_x()`, `esc_attr__()`, `esc_attr_e()`, `esc_attr_x()` | `_e(`, `__(` echoed | same |
| SEC-15 | "Nonces should never be relied on for authentication, authorization, or access control. Protect your functions using `current_user_can()`, and always assume nonces can be compromised." | handlers with a nonce but no capability check | https://developer.wordpress.org/apis/security/nonces/ |
| SEC-16 | `check_admin_referer()` "terminates execution with 403 response if verification fails"; `check_ajax_referer()` checks the nonce only, not the referrer, and dies by default; `wp_verify_nonce()` returns false so you must handle it. | `wp_verify_nonce(` without `if`; `check_ajax_referer( 'x', 'nonce', false )` with the result ignored | same |
| SEC-17 | Nonce creation: `wp_nonce_url()`, `wp_nonce_field()`, `wp_create_nonce()`. "By default, a nonce has a lifetime of one day." | action-specific nonce names (not one shared nonce for everything) | same |

## REST API permission rules
Sources: https://developer.wordpress.org/rest-api/extending-the-rest-api/adding-custom-endpoints/#permissions-callback , https://developer.wordpress.org/reference/functions/register_rest_route/ , https://developer.wordpress.org/rest-api/using-the-rest-api/authentication/ (fetched 2026-10-04).

| ID | Rule / check (quoted or the check's own message; shorten only by cutting, never by paraphrasing) | What to grep/read for in plugin code | Source URL |
|---|---|---|---|
| REST-1 | The permissions callback "checks if the user can perform the action (reading, updating, etc) before the real callback is called." | each `register_rest_route` method array has `'permission_callback' =>` | https://developer.wordpress.org/rest-api/extending-the-rest-api/adding-custom-endpoints/ |
| REST-2 | The guide stresses checking "whether they can perform the action (authorization)", not only authentication status. | `is_user_logged_in()` used as the permission; a capability weaker than the write (round 2: `manage_categories` guarding a write) | same |
| REST-3 | The callback "should return a boolean or a `WP_Error` instance." If false, "a default error message will be returned and the request will not proceed." | callbacks returning strings/arrays/ints | same |
| REST-4 | User note on register_rest_route: return "one of `false`, `null`, or a `WP_Error` if access is disallowed. Anything else, including other 'falsey' values, will grant access." | `return 0;`, `return '';`, `return array();` in permission callbacks: these GRANT access | https://developer.wordpress.org/reference/functions/register_rest_route/ |
| REST-5 | "For public routes, use __return_true." (`_doing_it_wrong` since 5.5.0: "The REST API route definition is missing the required permission_callback argument. For public routes, use __return_true.") | missing `permission_callback`; any `__return_true` on a route that writes or returns private data | same |
| REST-6 | `_doing_it_wrong` also fires for: "Routes must be namespaced with plugin or theme name and version." / "Namespace must not start or end with a slash." / "REST API routes must be registered on the rest_api_init action." | `register_rest_route(` outside a `rest_api_init` callback; namespace like `'vgml/v1'` (prefixed, versioned) | same |
| REST-7 | `$route_namespace`: "Should be unique to your package/plugin." | REST namespace prefix | same |
| REST-8 | Cookie auth: "The API uses nonces with the action set to `wp_rest`." Sent "via the `_wpnonce` data parameter… or via the `X-WP-Nonce` header." "If no nonce is provided the API will set the current user to 0, turning the request into an unauthenticated request". | JS `fetch` calls send `X-WP-Nonce: wpApiSettings.nonce` (`wp_create_nonce( 'wp_rest' )`) | https://developer.wordpress.org/rest-api/using-the-rest-api/authentication/ |
| REST-9 | "The current user must have the appropriate capability to perform the action being performed." "You do not need to verify that the nonce is valid inside your custom end point. This is automatically done for you in `rest_cookie_check_errors()`." | no manual `wp_verify_nonce` needed in REST callbacks, but a capability check is | same |
| REST-10 | Arguments: use `validate_callback` and `sanitize_callback`; validation "should return true if the value is valid, and false if not." | `'args' => array(` with `type`, `enum`, `sanitize_callback`, `validate_callback` for every param | https://developer.wordpress.org/rest-api/extending-the-rest-api/adding-custom-endpoints/ |
| REST-11 | Callbacks should "always return data; it shouldn't attempt to send the response body itself." Return `WP_REST_Response` or `WP_Error`; use `rest_ensure_response()`. | `echo`/`wp_send_json`/`exit` inside REST callbacks | same |
| REST-12 | Per-object authorization is part of "can perform the action". The release review flags "REST/AJAX/admin endpoints without capability checks". | routes taking an `id`: `current_user_can( 'edit_post', $id )` / `'delete_post'` rather than a global cap; attachment ids checked to be attachments | https://make.wordpress.org/plugins/2026/09/09/automated-security-review-for-plugin-releases/ |

Method note: GitHub sources were read from a git clone and raw files, not through WebFetch summaries, so those quotes are exact. developer.wordpress.org and make.wordpress.org texts went through WebFetch, which returns a model summary: sentences in quotation marks are the fetcher's verbatim extracts, while unquoted connecting text is its wording.

**Seed titles NOT verified** (no first-party public quote of them as review-email titles):
- "Check permission_callback" / "REST API permission_callback": known only from the caller's round-2 email.
- "Adding insecure MIME types": known only from the caller's round-2 email.
- "Nonces and user permissions" as a title (the rules themselves are verified).
- "Trialware / locked features" as an email title (guideline 5 verified).
- "Hiding admin notices" (guideline 11 verified, hiding other notices not found).
- "Tracking without consent" as an email title (guideline 7 + checklist verified).
- "Determine files and directories locations correctly" as a title (checklist item verified).
- "Uninstall" as a title (PC check verified).
- "Remote code / eval / base64" as a title (guideline 8 + PC ForbiddenFunctions verified).
- "Sessions": nothing found anywhere.
- "custom post types/taxonomies prefix" specifically (generic prefix rule verified).
- No wordpress.org support-forum thread quoting an AUTOPREREVIEW email was found. Searches for "Changing Active Plugins", "Adding insecure MIME types", "Check permission_callback", "Allowing Direct File Access to plugin files" and "Undocumented use of a 3rd Party" returned no forum threads.

UNFETCHED: none. The only failure was local `gh` CLI calls blocked by a hook, replaced with api.github.com fetches.
